Ground Truth report library

We Coded Tori Trades' Trendlines and Tested QQQ

Episode A30 · Full test report · Research summary, not trading instructions

1. The question and the answer: NO COMPLETE-OUTCOME VERDICT; INSUFFICIENT COVERAGE / PRECISION

NO COMPLETE-OUTCOME VERDICT; INSUFFICIENT COVERAGE / PRECISION

D00 pre-outcome selection, shared by natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

We tested whether our written trendline-bounce entries add value relative to later random entries with the same exits. The comparison does not establish added entry value; it also does not establish absence of value or equivalence. Tori Trades is the respected source of the setup. Tori's replay demonstrates decisions and profits, but supplies no numerical strategy win rate, expectancy or account-return record. Tori's skill, honesty, actual crude-oil trades, discretion and course remain untested.

Our rule searches long and short entries on SPY, QQQ and IWM. The realized matched sample is long QQQ only. This is a retrospective matched event comparison on surviving liquid funds; it is not randomized assignment, a portfolio backtest, a live fills test or a survivorship-free stock-universe study.

Sources: R:13-R:19; R:97; R:105; P:17-P:21; P:130; N:121-N:148. Source-ID meanings and hashes are below.

2. Opportunity: 24 long QQQ setups in 2016-2024, every annual count (zeros kept), the D00 funnel

D00 selected 24 long QQQ setups during 2016-2024. A setup is a pre-outcome entry opportunity, not a completed trade. The mean is 24/9, about three per year; the observed range is zero to eight. These counts predict no future year. Eighteen selected setups occurred in 2018, 2020 and 2022: this descriptive concentration after counting is not the separately defined set of eighteen matched donors. D00 pre-outcome selection, shared by natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

3. Our data rule removed SPY and IWM: anchor, gaps, rejection counts; our strictness, not a market or Tori verdict

D00 pre-outcome selection, shared by natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

The monthly low is dated 2011-10-04. In the long-entry examples, IWM has one unknown minute on 2013-01-08 and SPY has 29 on 2014-10-30 between the root anchor and signal. Our registered rule does not admit a ray across any unknown minute in that span. These are data-rule exclusions, not evidence that those markets have no setups or that Tori’s method fails. The calendar timeline is a schematic, not a price path.

FundPrior-admissible long sessionsFirst fail: monthly ATR warm-upFirst fail: anchor-span validityScopeSource

The two first-failure counts in each row sum to the prior-admissible long-session denominator. They exclude the 45 sessions without the prior regime, and they do not describe short-session failures. Short sessions also fail the registered starting-line requirements; F:31 and F:35 retain the different partitions. SPY and IWM remain in the coverage denominator with zero selected entries. The annual data-completeness gate passing does not make the monthly root valid.

Matching draws with replacement from the next 60 scheduled sessions, preserving fund, side and clock slot, with matched prior regime and translated donor geometry. The copied line preserves slope, clearance, initial stop distance and exit policy; it is a constructed reference, not a newly detected line at the random time. Twenty draws per matched donor yield 360 draws, but only 239 distinct twin paths; 121 draws repeat paths. These are not independent histories. Sources: P:130-P:159; R:104.

Pre-entry imbalance, shared by both named ledgers: the 18 line entries and 360 twin draws have mean D/ATR15 of 1.044149 and 1.532247, standardized difference -0.628743. Matching did not balance volatility-scaled exposure; the direction of bias is unknown. There was no rematching. D00 pre-outcome selection, shared by natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

Cut wording clarification: “Each line entry got twenty random twins” refers to each of the eighteen matched line entries, not to the six unmatched selections. The literal film sentence stays in the figure table beside this qualification.

5. The two ledgers: natural-exit primary (11 of 378 unresolved) and the finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit (0 of 378)

D00 natural-exit primary: 11 of 378 events remain unresolved at the data endpoint. D00 finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit: zero of 378 remain unresolved. The 378 events are eighteen line entries plus 360 twin draws, not 378 independent histories. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

The natural policy has protective/pivot-trail stops and manual closure at the next open after a completed 15-minute candle crosses the line. There is no break-even, profit-target or session-end exit. Natural follow-up runs through 2026-06-18. The companion marks a surviving entry at the next open after 63 sessions; it is our follow-up convention and can include a 64th-session opening execution. This endpoint is not a natural exit. The registered complete-outcome branch selects the companion for the descriptive comparison, but does not repair failed coverage. Sources: P:110-P:124; P:251-P:257; R:18-R:19.

6. The qualified estimate, only as its whole sentence; synthetic calibration 92.7% / 90.05%; dependence condition

Delta_block -1.40R, beside pooled donor-weighted Delta -0.913715R, is not a verdict: the 18 matched long QQQ setups, coverage fails, interval withheld, 6 of 8 required occupied blocks. D00; finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit; $42 risk / $2 cost. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

These are net-R line-minus-twin point differences. The pooled estimate weights matched donors equally; the block estimate weights occupied 248-session donor-entry blocks equally. Neither weighting establishes independence. The spoken approximation “about minus zero point nine one R” and displayed six-decimal pooled value are the same source estimate, with different rounding. The six occupied blocks fail the required eight; the registered 496-session sensitivity reading does not rescue that failure. Sources: R:97; R:111; P:208-P:222.

Synthetic calibration, not market evidence: 1854/2000 supplied histories = 92.7%, and 1801/2000 = 90.05%, with shared random numbers. These displayed rates concern the shared-shock .25 and .50 settings; the complete frozen battery is six scenarios, six passing the >=90% gate. The second rate passes the preset letter, not by a margin; Monte Carlo standard error is about 0.7 percentage points. Pre-run Amendment 3 permitted exactly one interval substitution on these same frozen scenarios; it did not authorize a method search. conditional on dependence not reaching beyond neighbouring 248-session blocks. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

SAMPLING CONFIDENCE WITHHELD applies to the market comparison despite the synthetic gate passing. Coverage and block support remain inadequate. No confidence range or efficacy verdict is supplied. Sources: R:111; R:13670; P:216-P:222.

The pooled mean, median and p95 below are magnitudes conditional on a negative net outcome; their denominator is loss events. Loss frequency uses all arm events. The two tail columns deliberately have different denominators. Twin rows count with-replacement draws, not independent trades.

Tail-containing blocks: line 0/6, twins 4/6; at least five tail-containing and eight occupied blocks were required. Line conditional loss statistics exist in only five of six occupied blocks, so the equal-block conditional-loss comparison is unestimable. Zero observed line losses beyond the cutoff cannot establish a zero population tail or a maximum possible loss. Sources: R:168; R:170; R:429; R:431; R:655; R:1212-R:1213.

Shared protective geometry constrains ordinary fills by construction, while costs and gaps can exceed planned risk. No otherwise identical no-stop arm was run. These loss counts do not isolate causal credit for exits, establish safety, or prove that entry policies are equivalent.

8. Exact costs: all nine risk/cost rows and the unidentifiable twin-relative requirement

The nine rows are assumed cost scenarios, not broker quotes or measured expectancies. Gross expectancy equal to C/R breaks even; it must exceed C/R to clear costs. These are exact fractions, not estimates with sampling intervals. D00; natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit. 10. Method, custody (attempts 1-3, amendments) and limitations

The private lock is f617d5d. Before that lock, Amendment 1 treated four official market-wide halts as known no-trade while vendor gaps stayed strict. Amendment 2 measured dependence reachability and activated fixed donor-entry blocks. Amendment 3, fb08ab1, allowed one interval substitution using the same frozen synthetic calibration. These pre-run exceptions were not selected from A30 market returns. The timestamp-only support audit preceded the lock; “before prices or outcomes” refers to this study’s market-price and outcome inspection, not blindness to Tori’s replay or to other research. Sources: P:7; P:44; P:65-P:75; P:212-P:224; N:180.

The accepted post-lock integration clarification c733d66 identifies the data as a stored split-adjusted snapshot: conversion factor one for three of three admitted funds under the pinned reference. It also records scheduling and timing corrections without changing the registered study. Reference completeness remains assumed. The data are regular-hours intraday price paths, not total returns; extended-session stop hits, dividends, financing, borrow costs and actual futures sessions are omitted. Fractional exposure and guessed round-trip costs preclude a literal-contract execution claim. Sources: R:9; N:182; P:118; P:141; P:163.

Administrative custody, not result samples: attempt one was void because an epoch-unit filter returned zero input rows for three funds. Attempt two was incomplete after required exit-subtype instrumentation was found missing; its partial outputs are not evidence here. Attempt three restarted the unchanged study from scratch and supplied the accepted result ledger at 442cdc2. Selection diagnostics at a95b54b were triggered by selection counts, with no exit-engine or outcome-file read; the researcher already knew the main result. This is not a claim of personal ignorance of outcomes. Sources: N:184-N:186.

11. Summary of the plan locked before the run, and what would change our answer

The invitation in the film is to “the full test report on our site”, “with the numbers”, “a summary of the plan we locked before the run”, and “what would change our answer”. The on-screen wording is “FULL TEST REPORTS + PLAN SUMMARIES”. This page and its downloads pay those A30 promises. The immediately following spoken sentence, “We are still filling in the back catalogue”, qualifies the channel’s publishing practice. Offline records identify 43 previously public episodes and 26 report files; seventeen historical episodes still lack one. This is not a claim that the archive is complete or a fresh HTTP check.

summary scope

Summary of the plan locked before the run. Summary, not the full plan text. Main terms only: not the complete private registry, raw event exports or a public immutable preregistration. The lock commit is in a private repository with no public ref that pins it; these hashes identify records, not public timestamp proof. Source wording about Tori is paraphrased without changing its meaning.

Sources: Summary scope notice

private lock refs

Lock a30-prereg-lock = f617d5d. Pre-lock amendments: 1 official market-wide halts as known no-trade; 2 measured dependence reachability and the donor-entry-block fallback; 3 exactly one interval substitution (fb08ab1) on the same frozen synthetic calibration. Accepted post-lock integration amendment c733d66 (price basis: stored split-adjusted snapshot, factor 1 for 3/3 funds). Attempt 1 void (0 input rows), attempt 2 incomplete (instrumentation), attempt 3 accepted (442cdc2). Private hashes identify records; they are not public proofs.

Sources: P:65; P:212; P:222; N:180-184

questions Q1/Q2/Q3 and film omission of Q3

Q1 ENTRIES: with the same mechanised exits, do trendline-bounce entries beat random entries on the same instrument, side, clock slot, regime and initial stop distance? Q2 LOSSES: how small are losses under each entry policy, and how much containment is already there with random entries and the same exits (no no-stop arm)? Q3 WHAT IT WOULD TAKE: exact gross R needed to clear the estimated costs, and the improvement over twins needed. The film states no cost row; Q3 is report-only.

Sources: P:17; P:19; P:21

universe, dates and data

SPY, QQQ, IWM (DIA dropped by the support rule); regular-session minute bars from the research data snapshot; warm-up 2011-04-01..2015-12-31; line entries 2016-01-01..2024-12-31; natural follow-up to 2026-06-18; XNYS calendar 4.13.2; 15-minute entry grid 09:45 to 15 minutes before close; line frames month, week, day, 4-hour, 1-hour, 15-minute.

Sources: P:31; P:33; P:34; P:36; P:38; P:39; P:40

data validity

Unknown minutes are never zero-return bars; no ray is valid across an unknown span from its anchor to the entry; "This strict validity may limit opportunity and is not to be relaxed after seeing counts or results."

Sources: P:46; P:48

line rule (ours)

A connected top-down cascade of lines through confirmed pivots; the touch tolerance, pivot confirmation, numeric entry delay and missing-minute contract are our choices, not the source's.

Sources: P:77; N:136

exits

Natural-exit primary: protective / pivot-trail stop, or a completed 15-minute close through the frozen line with manual closure at the next open; no break-even, profit-target or session-end exit. Finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit: marked at the next open after 63 sessions, a separate follow-up estimand.

Sources: P:110; P:112; P:114; P:122; P:124

random-entry twins and balance

K=20 twins per matched donor from the next 60 scheduled sessions at the same clock slot, same fund and side, matched trend and volatility buckets, a translated donor line with the same stop distance; fixed seeds; balance printed, no rematching.

Sources: P:130; P:134; P:136; P:137; P:145; P:151; P:157

sizing and costs

R = planned initial dollar risk ($42, $50, $420), never an account percentage or a loss ceiling; estimated round-trip costs are guesses swept x0/x1/x2: 9 accounting cells; headline $42 / $2.

Sources: P:163; P:165; P:169; P:173

coverage gates

>=90% of selected donors matched; >=500 matched groups; >=50 per fund; >=24 represented quarters; every entry year <=5% incomplete sessions; failure = INSUFFICIENT COVERAGE / PRECISION for Q1/Q2 while Q3 still prints.

Sources: P:153; P:54

dependence and intervals

Fixed 248-session donor-entry blocks; >=8 occupied blocks, a 496-session reading check and a frozen calibration >=90% in every scenario, else SAMPLING CONFIDENCE WITHHELD; every interval "conditional on dependence not reaching beyond neighbouring 248-session blocks".

Sources: P:214; P:216; P:218; P:219; P:220

registered decision rules

Ordered statuses: complete coverage and outcomes first; otherwise NO COMPLETE-OUTCOME VERDICT with selected, matched and unresolved counts retained; Q1 labels only through the validity gates; a crossing of zero never proves equivalence (.05R margin).

Sources: P:246; P:252; P:261; P:266

other designs

D00 is the registered primary rule. D01-D11 are the eleven registered one-change designs; their selected-session counts are reported, without outcome rankings or a performance vote. D09-D11 change exits and inherit D00 entry timing. These are pre-outcome counts shared by natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

Sources: P:177; F:95

prior expectation

A guess fixed before the run: the point difference may be within .05R; a guess, not a result.

Sources: P:282

what would change our answer

A separately registered missing-data contract and line rule with enough opportunity; enough complete, dispersed matched groups under a named exit or follow-up rule that pass the block and calibration gates; crude-oil paths with an independently specified, source-faithful decision and execution record; or a supported source correction / reproducible code defect for the affected claim. A nicer-looking result from another version of the rule is not one.

Sources: N:188-194

terms

A donor is a selected line-entry setup; a matched donor has an admissible twin pool. Only matched donors receive the twenty-draw bundle. A twin is one with-replacement later entry draw carrying a translated version of that donor geometry. K is the fixed number of twins per matched donor, twenty. Delta is line-entry net R minus the mean of its twins, then averaged across donors; Delta_block gives occupied blocks equal weight. R is fixed initial planned dollar risk, not account return or a loss ceiling. A block is a fixed calendar group of 248 scheduled sessions, assigned by donor entry; it is not an assertion of independent history. D00 coverage gates are matched fraction, matched group count, count per admitted fund, represented quarters, and completeness of each admitted fund-year.

Sources: P:130; P:145; P:153; P:163; P:208; P:214

separate result and interval identities

The natural-exit primary and finite-horizon companion, not Tori's exit are separate outcome definitions. INSUFFICIENT COVERAGE / PRECISION and NO COMPLETE-OUTCOME VERDICT govern the registered decision. TAIL PRECISION UNRESOLVED is a loss-tail support status; synthetic calibration is not market evidence. conditional on dependence not reaching beyond neighbouring 248-session blocks. Our mechanical reading, on real intraday paths of liquid US instruments; not Tori's crude-oil market or a reconstruction of Tori's discretionary trades.

Sources: P:216; P:246; P:252; P:272

12. Every film figure with its source; downloads

This inventory covers 109 spoken sentences in thirteen voice segments, 324 distinct drawn text/role/context placements representing 821 state appearances, and eleven raster-text groups: 444 rows. Its screen denominator is 69 slides and 174 states, including focus states. Repeated draws are coalesced only when slide, role, words and evidence context agree; their state identifiers remain listed. A raster group keeps several adjacent interface labels together with their shared meaning.

The sixteen separately inspected raster placements are three attributed source cards, two uses of the same real QQQ crop, one plan excerpt and ten mascot-only cutaways. The recurring mascot layer has 174 state placements, including those ten cutaways; each reuses one of two face assets, both without numerals or word-figures. The QQQ crop has no numeric axes or return labels. The cutaways carry no numerals or word-figures. The two source chart crops also show small prices, dates, replay-speed and interval controls; those are source interface values, not A30 measurements. Obscured header fields, the obscured sell quote and the clock offset hidden by the portrait are not legible at 1080p and are not inferred. Figure rows retain the actual frame coordinates. The private plan excerpt is a brief quoted sentence; the download remains a summary, not the full plan text.

VOICE-B03-01spoken sentence; B03 sentence 1In the trendline video, Tori Trades shows a crude-oil replay, a demonstration of Tori's decisions.In the trendline video, Tori Trades shows a crude-oil replay, a demonstration of Tori's decisions. Tori is a respected source, and the replay shows profits. Like any replay, it shows the method rather than a win rate, expected return or account record. Tori stays with the trend while price holds the line, and exits through a protective stop, or by hand once a completed candle closes across the line. A candle is one bar on the chart. In Tori's words, it doesn't matter if it comes back to break even. A comparison asks whether entry times add anything when the exit rules stay the same. VOICE-B08-01spoken sentence; B08 sentence 1Comparable entries are needed to separate timing from the exits that follow.Comparable entries are needed to separate timing from the exits that follow. Each line entry got twenty random twins, later times drawn with repeats from the next sixty trading days. Each twin shared the entry's fund, direction and time of day, in similar prior market conditions. Each twin copied the line's slope, clearance and initial stop distance for its exits. That copy was a constructed reference, not a line found at the random time. Matching still left unequal exposure to recent price swings, and we cannot tell which way that tilted the result. Our registered plan set five coverage gates. Six of the twenty-four found no match, leaving eighteen, below the required ninety percent. Those eighteen setups and their twins made eighteen groups, against five hundred required. Each fund needed fifty groups, and even QQQ fell short. Thirteen calendar quarters were represented, below the required twenty-four. Only the data-completeness check passed, since nearly every day's record was complete, leaving one of five coverage gates satisfied. The next hurdle is knowing how each comparison ended.P:77; P:78; P:79; P:80; P:81; P:82; P:83; P:84; P:85; P:86; P:87; P:88; P:89; P:90; P:91; P:92; P:93; P:94; P:95; P:96; P:97; P:98; P:99; P:100; P:101; P:102; P:103; P:104; P:105; P:106; P:107; P:108; P:109; P:110; P:111; P:112; P:113; P:114; P:115; P:116; N:130; N:131; N:132; N:133; N:134; N:135; N:136; N:137; N:138; N:139; N:140; N:141; N:142; N:143; N:144; N:145; N:146; P:7; P:44Our registered numerical definition of the setup; a mechanical proxy, not a reconstruction of discretionary oil trades. The 63-session companion is our follow-up convention.

Film CSV columns: figure_id identifies one row; medium distinguishes spoken sentence, drawn text and source raster; location records beat/sentence or slide/role/states or frame; words retains literal cut text; context supplies its full spoken beat, board or raster meaning; source_ids names the supporting records; evidence_contexts names the distinct evidence meanings; provenance explains conditions and any labelled clarification. These are three different row types, not independent measured observations. The source index columns are source_id, meaning, sha256_lf and reference: a semantic identifier, source description, hash of LF-normalized text or original binary pixels, and a private commit or public external URL. A pixel hash is not a text hash. Neither index publishes the private source files.

Ground Truth is educational and entertainment content - not financial, investment, or tax advice. We are not licensed or registered advisers; we share original research and opinions. Nothing here is a recommendation to buy, sell, or hold any security, fund, or strategy. Backtests and simulations are hypothetical, model real-world costs imperfectly, and past performance does not guarantee future results. Do your own research and consult a qualified professional before making any financial decision.
